CARTA DE RECOMENDACAO 
Declaro para os devidos fins que a Sra. Marina de Aratijo Jacob RG N° 
961.600 SSP-DF a pessoa de meu conhecimento, idonea, correta, 
competente, responsavel e pontual, tendo trabalhado na Embaixada da 
RepOblica do Azerbaijao, em Brasilia, exercendo o cargo de faxineira 
desde o inicio de 2018, nao constando registros de nada que a 
desabone, mantendo ao longo do vinculo conduta pessoal e 
professional irrepreensiveis. 
Brasilia / DF, 28 de dezembro de 2018 
RECOMENDATION LETTER 
This is to inform that Mrs. Marina de AraUjo Jacob RG N° 961,600 SSP-
DF is a person of my knowledge, correct, competent, responsible and 
timely, having worked at the Embassy of the Republic of Azerbaijan in 
Brasilia, in the position of cleaner since the beginning of 2018, not 
containing records of anything against her, maintaining along a great 
personal and professional relationship as an excellent cleaner. 
Brasilia / DF, December 28"-', 2018. 
SE Sr. Elkhan Polukhov 
Embaixador da Republica do Azerbaijao no Brasil 
Ambassador of the Republic of Azerbaijan to Brazil
